Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a proofs algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 457. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 855/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 457 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the proofs structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 855. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the proofs structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 637. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 648 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the proofs decomposition. By minimality, the element a^637 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 648-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
